  CanonicalStructure.IsCanonical →
    M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF00 →
      ∀ {κ : Cardinal.{0}},
        SpecialTransformations.Section3.Saturated M κ →
          ∀ (S : Set M),
            Cardinal.mk ↑S < κ →
              DiamondIntegral.Section2.NontrivialDefinableGamma { real := S, gamma := ∅ } →
                (∀ (E : DiamondIntegral.Section2.ParameterBase M), DiamondIntegral.Section2.UnaryDiscAtomicityInput E) →
                  (∀ (E : DiamondIntegral.Section2.ParameterBase M),
                      DiamondIntegral.Section2.UnaryOpenDiscRVBaseChangeInput E) →
                    (∀ (E : DiamondIntegral.Section2.ParameterBase M),
                        DiamondIntegral.Section2.OpenDiscNamesImageInput E) →
                      ∀ {n : ℕ} (A : Set (Fin n → M)),
                        SpecialTransformations.Section3.DefinableOver S
                            (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.vf) A →
                          ∀ (F : Set ((Fin n → M) × M)),
                            SpecialTransformations.Section3.DefinableOver S
                                (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.vf)
                                {z | (Fin.init z, z (Fin.last n)) ∈ F} →
                              (∀ x ∈ A, {y | (x, y) ∈ F}.Finite) →
                                (∀ x ∈ A, Set.InjOn SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRv {y | (x, y) ∈ F}) →
                                  ∃ Q,
                                    Q.Finite ∧
                                      Q.PairwiseDisjoint id ∧
                                        (∀ V ∈ Q,
                                            V.Nonempty ∧
                                              V ⊆ A ∧
                                                SpecialTransformations.Section3.DefinableOver S
                                                    (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.vf) V ∧
                                                  ∀ (a : Fin n → DiamondIntegral.Section2.VF M)
                                                    (δ : Fin n → DiamondIntegral.Section2.Gamma M),
                                                    DiamondIntegral.Section2.namedPolydisc (fun x => false) a δ ⊆ V →
                                                      DiamondIntegral.Section2.TaggedPolydiscBranchDecomposition
                                                        { real := S, gamma := ∅ } a δ F) ∧
                                          ∀ x ∈ A, ∃ V ∈ Q, x ∈ V

    M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF00 →
      ∀ {κ : Cardinal.{0}},
        SpecialTransformations.Section3.Saturated M κ →
          Cardinal.aleph0 ≤ κ →
            ∀ (S : Set M),
              Cardinal.mk ↑S < κ →
                DiamondIntegral.Section2.NontrivialDefinableGamma { real := S, gamma := ∅ } →
                  (∀ (E : DiamondIntegral.Section2.ParameterBase M),
                      DiamondIntegral.Section2.UnaryDiscAtomicityInput E) →
                    (∀ (E : DiamondIntegral.Section2.ParameterBase M),
                        DiamondIntegral.Section2.AtomicDiscGammaBaseChangeInput E) →

                          DiamondIntegral.Section2.UnaryOpenDiscRVBaseChangeInput E) →
                        (∀ (E : DiamondIntegral.Section2.ParameterBase M),
                            DiamondIntegral.Section2.OpenDiscNamesImageInput E) →
                          ∀ {k n : ℕ} (ground : Fin k → Bool) (b : Fin k → DiamondIntegral.Section2.VF M)
                            (τ : Fin k → DiamondIntegral.Section2.Gamma M) (A : Set (Fin n → M)),
                            DiamondIntegral.Section2.DefinableOverFiniteDiscNames { real := S, gamma := ∅ } ground b τ
                                (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.vf) A →
                              ∀ (F : Set ((Fin n → M) × M)),
                                DiamondIntegral.Section2.DefinableOverFiniteDiscNames { real := S, gamma := ∅ } ground b
                                    τ (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.vf)
                                    {z | (Fin.init z, z (Fin.last n)) ∈ F} →
                                  (∀ x ∈ A, {y | (x, y) ∈ F}.Finite) →
                                    (∀ x ∈ A, Set.InjOn SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRv {y | (x, y) ∈ F}) →
                                      ∃ Q,
                                        Q.Finite ∧
                                          Q.PairwiseDisjoint id ∧
                                            (∀ V ∈ Q,
                                                V.Nonempty ∧
                                                  V ⊆ A ∧
                                                    DiamondIntegral.Section2.DefinableOverFiniteDiscNames
                                                        { real := S, gamma := ∅ } ground b τ
                                                        (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.vf) V ∧

                                                            V →
                                                          DiamondIntegral.Section2.TaggedPolydiscBranchDecompositionOverDiscNames

  [inst_1 : LinearOrderedCommGroupWithZero G] [inst_2 : Valued (DiamondIntegral.Section2.VF M) G],

    CanonicalStructure.IsCanonical →
      ∀ {κ : Cardinal.{0}},
        Cardinal.aleph0 < κ →
          SpecialTransformations.Section3.Saturated M κ →
            (∀ (x y : DiamondIntegral.Section2.VF M),
                SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.VFValued.predV x y ↔ Valued.v x ≤ Valued.v y) →
              ∀ {S : Set M} (A : Set (Fin n → DiamondIntegral.Section2.VF M)),
                S.Definable SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV (SpecialTransformations.Section4.vfValues '' A) →
                  IsClosed A →
                    (∃ γ, ∀ x ∈ A, ∀ (i : Fin n), ↑γ ≤ DiamondIntegral.Section2.vfValue (x i)) →
                      ∀ (f : (Fin n → DiamondIntegral.Section2.VF M) → Fin m → DiamondIntegral.Section2.VF M),
                        S.Definable SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV
                            (DiamondIntegral.Section2.vfCorrespondenceGraph A fun x => {f x}) →
                          ContinuousOn f A → Set.InjOn f A → ∃ H, ∀ (x : ↑A), ↑(H x) = f ↑x

  [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] (S : Set M) {n m k : ℕ}

      ∀ {κ : Cardinal.{0}},
        Cardinal.aleph0 < κ →
          SpecialTransformations.Section3.Saturated M κ →
            (∀ (x y : DiamondIntegral.Section2.VF M),
                SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.VFValued.predV x y ↔ Valued.v x ≤ Valued.v y) →
              ∀ {S : Set M} (A : Set (Fin n → DiamondIntegral.Section2.VF M)),
                S.Definable SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV (SpecialTransformations.Section4.vfValues '' A) →
                  ∀ (B : Set (Fin m → DiamondIntegral.Section2.VF M)),
                    S.Definable SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV (SpecialTransformations.Section4.vfValues '' B) →

                        ∀ (f : (Fin n → DiamondIntegral.Section2.VF M) → Fin m → DiamondIntegral.Section2.VF M),

                            S.Definable SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV
                                (DiamondIntegral.Section2.vfCorrespondenceGraph A fun x => {f x}) →

@DiamondIntegral.Section2.lemma_2_51 : ∀ {M : Type} [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M]
  [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {G : Type} [inst_1 : LinearOrderedCommGroupWithZero G]
  [inst_2 : Valued (DiamondIntegral.Section2.VF M) G],
  CanonicalStructure.IsCanonical →
    ∀ {κ : Cardinal.{0}},
      Cardinal.aleph0 ≤ κ →
        SpecialTransformations.Section3.Saturated M κ →
          ∀ {S : Set M},
            Cardinal.mk ↑S < κ →
              (∀ (x y : DiamondIntegral.Section2.VF M),
                  SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.VFValued.predV x y ↔ Valued.v x ≤ Valued.v y) →
                ∀ (A B : Set (DiamondIntegral.Section2.VF M)),
                  IsOpen A →
                    IsOpen B →
                      ∀ (f : DiamondIntegral.Section2.VF M → DiamondIntegral.Section2.VF M),
                        Set.BijOn f A B →
                          S.Definable SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV
                              (DiamondIntegral.Section2.scalarPartialGraph A f) →
                            (∃ γ, ∀ y ∈ B, ↑γ ≤ DiamondIntegral.Section2.vfValue y) →
                              ∀ a ∈ A,
                                ∃ b γ₀,
                                  ∀ (γ : DiamondIntegral.Section2.Gamma M),
                                    γ₀ ≤ γ →
                                      ∃ δ,
                                        f '' DiamondIntegral.Section2.puncturedGammaDisc a γ =
                                          DiamondIntegral.Section2.puncturedGammaDisc b δ

    ∀ {κ : Cardinal.{0}} (hκ : Cardinal.aleph0 ≤ κ) (hs : SpecialTransformations.Section3.Saturated M κ)

      (hγ : Cardinal.mk ↑B.gamma < κ),

        ∀ (k : DiamondIntegral.Section4.EulerKind) (n m : ℕ)

          (hI : DiamondIntegral.Section4.GammaOrderLogic.GammaDefinable B I)
          (J : Set (Fin n → SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.Gamma))
          (hJ : DiamondIntegral.Section4.GammaOrderLogic.GammaDefinable B J)
          (μ : (Fin m → SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.Gamma) → SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.Gamma)
          (hμ : DiamondIntegral.Section4.GammaDefinableMaps.MapDefinable B μ)
          (σ : (Fin n → SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.Gamma) → SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.Gamma)
          (hσ : DiamondIntegral.Section4.GammaDefinableMaps.MapDefinable B σ)
          (φ :
            (Fin m → SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.Gamma) →
              Fin n → SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.Gamma),
          DiamondIntegral.Section4.GammaOrderLogic.GammaDefinable B
              (DiamondIntegral.Section4.GammaBijectiveRelations.graphOn I φ) →
            Set.BijOn φ I J →
              (∀ u ∈ I, μ u = (σ (φ u) * ∏ i, φ u i) / ∏ i, u i) →
                DiamondIntegral.Section4.GammaWeightProfiles.profile h00 hκ hs B hr hγ k I hI μ hμ =
                  DiamondIntegral.Section4.GammaWeightProfiles.profile h00 hκ hs B hr hγ k J hJ σ hσ

                    DiamondIntegral.Section2.NontrivialDefinableGamma { real := S, gamma := ∅ } →
                      (∀ a ∈ V, SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iVF a) →
                        V ⊆ S →
                          S ⊆ SpecialTransformations.Section3.algebraicClosureSet V →
                            (∃ x, x ≠ 0 ∧ Valued.v ↑x ≠ 1) →
                              ∀
                                (A :

                                    (SpecialTransformations.Section4.rawMixedSpace n r) S),
                                DiamondIntegral.Section6.ProperInvariant S A.carrier →
                                  ∀ (e : Equiv.Perm (Fin n)),
                                    Nonempty (DiamondIntegral.Section6.StandardContraction A e)

  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M]
  [inst_1 : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {r s : ℕ} {G : Type}

                        DiamondIntegral.Section6.ProperCovariantHomeomorphism f →
                          DiamondIntegral.Section6.ProperInvariant (↑S) A.carrier →
                            DiamondIntegral.Section6.ProperInvariant (↑S) B.carrier →

                                  SpecialTransformations.DefinableGeometry.Map A
                                    (SpecialTransformations.DefinableGeometry.DefSet.univ
                                      DiamondIntegral.Section6.rawGammaPresentation ↑S))

                                  SpecialTransformations.DefinableGeometry.Map B
                                    (SpecialTransformations.DefinableGeometry.DefSet.univ

                                (∀ (u : (SpecialTransformations.Section4.rawRVSpace r).Carrier)
                                    (γ : DiamondIntegral.Section2.Gamma M),
                                    IsOpen
                                      {a |
                                        (a, u) ∈ A.carrier ∧
                                          DiamondIntegral.Section6.gammaFormExtension ω (a, u) = γ}) →

                                      (γ : DiamondIntegral.Section2.Gamma M),
                                      IsOpen

                                          (a, u) ∈ B.carrier ∧
                                            DiamondIntegral.Section6.gammaFormExtension σ (a, u) = γ}) →

                                                    DiamondIntegral.Section6.gammaMapValue σ (f.toFun a))) →
                                      ∃ H,
                                        DiamondIntegral.Section6.RelativeApertureChain A H.geometry.source
                                            H.geometry.sourceMap ∧
                                          DiamondIntegral.Section6.RelativeApertureChain B H.geometry.target
                                            H.geometry.targetMap

@SpecialTransformations.Section5.theorem_5_5_finite : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {S : Set M} {n m k : ℕ} {Γ : Type} [inst : LinearOrderedCommGroupWithZero Γ],
  M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF00 →
    ∀ (v : Valuation SpecialTransformations.Section5.VF Γ),
      (∀ (x y : SpecialTransformations.Section5.VF), SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.VFValued.predV x y ↔ v x ≤ v y) →
        (∀ (N : ℕ), SpecialTransformations.Section5.UniformVerticalObstacles v S N) →
          ∀ {A₀ : SpecialTransformations.Section5.DefSet S n 0} {A : SpecialTransformations.Section5.DefSet S n m}
            (τ : SpecialTransformations.Section5.SpecialBijection A₀ A)
            (p : Fin k → MvPolynomial (Fin n) SpecialTransformations.Section5.VF),
            (∀ (i : Fin k) (d : Fin n →₀ ℕ), ↑((p i).coeff d) ∈ S) →
              ∃ r B T,
                SpecialTransformations.Section5.Chain.Alternating none (τ.chain.append T.chain) ∧
                  SpecialTransformations.Section5.IsRVPullback (SpecialTransformations.Section5.carrier B) ∧
                    ∀ (b c : ↑B.carrier),
                      SpecialTransformations.Section5.SameRV ↑b ↑c →
                        ∀ (i : Fin k),
                          SpecialTransformations.Section5.rv
                              ((MvPolynomial.eval
                                  (SpecialTransformations.Section5.vfAt ↑(τ.inverseMap.toFun (T.inverseMap.toFun b))))
                                (p i)) =
                            SpecialTransformations.Section5.rv
                              ((MvPolynomial.eval
                                  (SpecialTransformations.Section5.vfAt ↑(τ.inverseMap.toFun (T.inverseMap.toFun c))))
                                (p i))

  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]

  SpecialTransformations.Section3.Saturated M κ →
    M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF00 →
      ∀ {S : Set M},
        Cardinal.mk ↑S < κ →
          ∀ {n m : ℕ} {A₀ : SpecialTransformations.Section5.DefSet S n 0}
            {A : SpecialTransformations.Section5.DefSet S n m}
            (τ : SpecialTransformations.Section5.SpecialBijection A₀ A)

              ∃ r B T,

                              p)
